# What to install (current versions)

Updated 2026-11-27. Every file is in the `dist/` folder (it syncs through OneDrive, so the phone app has it too).
In Tampermonkey's dashboard, compare each script's version with this list. Anything already at these versions is up to date.

| Script | File in `dist/` | Where it runs | Current |
|---|---|---|---|
| Farmhand Bot | `farmhand-bot.user.js` | the bot's PC (account 260239) | **0.105.0** |
| Glory stalls | `farmhand-glory-stalls.user.js` | the bot's PC | **1.10.2** |
| Breeding | `farmhand-breeding.user.js` | the bot's PC | **1.4.0** |
| Conditioning | `farmhand-conditioning.user.js` | the bot's PC | **2.8.0** |
| Barn life | `farmhand-barn-life.user.js` | the bot's PC | **1.4.3** |
| Map tools, Work | `farmhand-<name>.user.js` | the bot's PC | **1.0.5** |
| Dairy | `farmhand-dairy.user.js` | the bot's PC | **1.1.2** |
| Shows | `farmhand-shows.user.js` | the bot's PC | **1.1.2** |
| Farmhand Companion | `farmhand-companion.user.js` | each player, and the bot's own tab | **0.91.1** |
| The Farmhand's Dispensary | `farmhand-dispensary.user.js` | any player who wants doses from crafted items, on the farm or off it | **0.29.1** |
| Farm Watcher (diagnostics) | `farm-watcher.user.js` | whoever is recording | **1.0.4** |

## Sending a watch report

- Recorded on the bot's PC: it lands in that PC's Downloads folder. Just tell Claude "new report" and it reads the newest one.
- Recorded on a phone or another computer: attach the `.txt` file in the chat.
- Running the watcher on more than one game at once (the bot, yours, a tester's) lets Claude line them up into one timeline.

## Latest changes (newest first)

- **Companion 0.91.1 + Dispensary 0.29.1: womb tattoos sit higher, chest ink rides up with bigger breasts** Sera's report, passed on by Laynie, Nov 26-27: "the knotslave tattoo, for instance sits too low, and part of those tattoos are not easily visible. the others seem fine as long as they are in their relative positions when the breast and stuff scale up". Laynie on the mock-up: "its a improvement".
  - **The womb's designs** (all 31: Knot Slave, Kitten Factory, the crests, Cum Here, 25¢ Per Ride, the live words and the rest) sit higher, every one's foot on the same line just over the slit, so none of it slides down between the thighs any more. The tallest (Knot Slave, Kitten Factory, Womb Crest and the like) are drawn a touch smaller so they fit the spot. Anything you've resized yourself in the ink panel keeps your size on top.
  - **Chest ink** (the chest designs, Broken In and Locked over the heart) rides up 6 for Large breasts and 12 for XLarge, so it stays on the chest above them, not slid down across them. Small and Normal are as they were.

- **Bot 0.105.0 + Companion 0.91.0: progressive restraints, and gear the farm leaves off you** Sera's ideas, passed on by Laynie, Nov 26: "traps and wild traps doing 'progressive restraints', which is to say the equipment builds a outfit over several traps", and "a way to disable hoods, or certain types of restraints". Laynie: "for the species, or atleast themed and complimenting their species and gender", how it comes off "iii toggleable", and the leave-off list "seems good".
  - **Progressive restraints** (Farm switches › Traps and play, or `?traps build on|off`; off till switched on, and only with "Farm can put real gear on me"): every trap that lets them go leaves the next piece of an outfit, a wild trap two, and the pieces stay and add up, light to heavy, the collar first and the hood last, with a line for each. The outfit suits their kind and body: **dairy tack** (cows, bulls, goats, sheep, pigs: dairy collar, cowbell, nose ring, cuffs, hobbles, hoof mitts, udder cups or a bull's lock, a cow's tail, a feed bit, a heifer hood), **pony tack** (posture collar, harness, hoof boots and mitts, bridle bit, a mare's tail or a stallion's, show bells or a lock, armbinder, pony hood), **kennel gear** (collar, bone tag, lead, paw mitts, muzzle, pup tail, bells or a lock, pup pads, dog hood), **kitty kit**, **hutch gear** for bunnies and rats, and **rubber** for everyone else. A tail never goes over a tail of their own. **How it comes off is their pick** (three buttons under the switch, or `?traps build free|hour|both`): only when somebody else frees them, piece by piece; an hour after the last piece if no trap adds another; or whichever comes first (the default). The whole set on: a closin' line, and it holds till it's come off, then the next trap starts a fresh one. Their safeword, `?traps build off` or `?traps off` take it all off. No other trap's gear pushes a piece of the set out of its spot. (A "Fully Fitted" trophy needs a tattoo design of its own; it comes with the tattoo work.)
  - **Gear the farm leaves off you** (Settings › ⛓ Gear and marks): tick any of hoods; gags (the feed funnel too); blindfolds and blinders; earplugs; arm restraints; leg restraints; plugs and toys; milkin' cups, nipple clamps and funnels; chastity belts; collars, leashes and tags; suits, harnesses and latex; costume bits; devices. The farm never puts a ticked kind on you, from a trap, a wild kit, a stall or a progressive set (a set skips to its next piece), and anything of that kind on you comes off at once. Your Companion holds to it too, even with a farm girl that hasn't heard yet.

- **Bot 0.104.0 + Companion 0.90.0: traps take clothes; the hypno effects step aside off the room screen; the visor's 👁 on the map**
  - **Traps take clothes** (Laynie, Nov 26: "traps should be more liberal with stripping clothing, like for penetrative events or ones that mention stripping", then "yes" to the plan). Every trap's lines are read as they're told (the wild traps' too): a line that says clothes come off takes everything; somethin' goin' into them from below takes their bottoms, underwear and whatever covers them (skirts, trousers, panties, dresses, outfits, suits, coats); their udders, teats or nipples bein' used takes their top and bra. Once each per trap, and a short line goes on the end of the trap's own namin' what went ("Moo's jeans and panties? Torn off and tossed over the rail."). Only with "Farm can strip me" on (now told to the farm girl when it's switched), never with strippin' or nudity in their trap limits; locked pieces, ears, tails, wings and the farm's marks stay on. Their clothes are kept on their own computer, and nothin' puts them back for them: the new "👗 My clothes back on" button by "Farm can strip me" does (or the game's wardrobe). Checked against all 8,233 trap lines: a plow, a trough, the top of a well, a needle, somebody else's tits, a fleece, the dairy's strippin' cups and lines that only push clothes aside take nothin'.
  - **The hypno effects step aside** (Sera's suggestion, passed on by Laynie: "The hypno effects should hide when an accounts profile page, or preference windows are open."). The farm's trance and visor show on the chat room screen only: on a profile, in the preferences (the farm's own settings and LSCG's among them), the wardrobe, the room's settings, or anywhere else, the conditioning haze, the trance look, the pull's nudges, and the visor's dark, afterimage, spiral over the chat, 🌀 and peek window are hidden, and come back the moment you're in the room again. Sound and sessions carry on underneath. (A visor's dark no longer covers, or blocks clicks on, the left half of the preferences.)
  - **The visor's 👁 and glow on the map too** (Laynie, Nov 26: "i dont see the button for peeking."). The glow, how deep and the 👁 were only drawn in the room's character view, and the farm's rooms are map rooms. They now ride on the game's own character drawin', so they're over a visored player in both views (on the room screen, never while somebody's dialog is open); on the map the 👁 keeps a size you can see and click. `/visor peek <name>` opens the window too. And a farm visor's dark now covers the map however wide it's been dragged out, not just the left half.

- **Companion 0.89.0 + Conditioning 2.8.0: the visor in the room: seen from outside, peekin' in, herd trance, the crafter loomin'; afterimages the crafter's pick** Laynie, Nov 25-26: from the visor ideas she picked "5-8" and said "sounds good"; and about the afterimage she didn't expect ("i have a spiral super imposed on my screen, even with no visor"), "yes" to makin' it the crafter's pick with a wearer's own switch.
  - **Afterimages**: only a visor whose crafter ticked "an afterimage at the edges…" in the Visor maker leaves one now (visors made before don't). And any wearer can switch them off on the trance card ("Visor afterimages"), which also clears one that's showin'.
  - **Seen from outside**: while a farm visor has somebody, everybody else with the Companion sees a slow pink glow over their eyes, how deep they are (🌀 60) over their head and a small 👁 beside it, in the room's character view (the map view gets the rest), and their lines in the chat tinted pink. Now and then (8 to 12 minutes apart) a line about them goes to the room as their own emote, so people without the Companion see it too ("{n}'s visor pulses slow and pink. Their mouth hangs a little open."); "Visor lines in the room" on the trance card switches that off.
  - **Peek inside**: a click on the 👁 opens a little window into their visor: the spiral, the body they see, and the picture, word and line showin' right now (silent videos too). The wearer's told who's lookin' in. Three peekers at a time, and what's sent is small and only to the peekers (every 4 seconds at most). If the crafter picked it in the maker ("me and their keyholders", or "anybody in the room"), a peeker can feed a word into their dark: it's the next word they see. One every 30 seconds each; only letters and numbers get through.
  - **Herd trance**: everybody in the room wearin' the same visor (the same crafter, the same code) shares one session. The lowest member number leads; the rest follow it line by line, each line in their own name, with a welcome for the herd at the start. Their bodies stand side by side in each other's dark, and they sink together. Each still earns their own sessions toward their own lock. If the leader goes quiet for a minute, the rest go back to their own sessions.
  - **The crafter looms**: two new choices for whose body they see: the crafter towerin' with them small at the crafter's feet, or their own body with the crafter's face hoverin' faint over them in the spiral, watchin'. When the crafter isn't in the room, the visor uses a small likeness of them it kept the last time they were (on the wearer's computer only, three crafters at most).
  - Fixed: a visor taken off in the few seconds right after a session ended left that session's trance runnin' till it timed out; it's woken now.

- **Companion 0.88.0 + Conditioning 2.7.0: movin' pictures in the visor: GIFs and silent videos, a link fixer, a kaleidoscope that keeps them movin', and Test my links** Laynie, Nov 26: "is there a way to make animated gifs work for the visor?", then "all four".
  - **Silent videos count as pictures**: a link straight to an .mp4 or .webm plays muted and loopin' in the picture's place, see-through like any picture, never a sound and no controls. Most GIF sites send video now (Giphy, Tenor, Imgur, RedGIFs), so this is what makes most of them move. Only the one showin' plays; the one that's faded out is stopped and let go of, so it stays light.
  - **The link fixer** (the Visor maker and the studio's picture sets): a Giphy page or embed link is made into its GIF, an Imgur .gifv into its video, a Google image-result link into the picture's own address. A link it can't work out (a Tenor or RedGIFs page, an Imgur album, a Reddit page) is left out with a plain word on what to copy instead, and Google thumbnails (always still) and Discord links (they stop working) get a warnin'. Their own list in the maker is fixed for them.
  - **The kaleidoscope keeps them movin'**: it's now six folded copies of the picture itself, so a GIF keeps movin' in every fold (a canvas only ever gets a GIF's first frame). A video's drawn from its own frames instead (six videos at once would be too heavy).
  - **Test my links** in the Visor maker: each of their own links tried, and said plainly: ✔ moves, ▫ a still picture, ✔ shows (where the site won't say whether it moves), or ✘ didn't load, and why.
  - The studio's picture picker shows .mp4 and .webm files from `public/visor/pictures/` too.

- **Companion 0.87.1: turn a farm visor back on without takin' it off** Laynie, Nov 26: "need to add a action on the visors so can turn it back on if they want, without re equipping." While a farm visor's worn but switched off (its 👁 icon, or the safeword), a small dim 🌀 sits in the same corner of the room: click it, or type `/visor on`, and it takes hold again. It's always the wearer's own choice; nothin' asks them to. In the Dispensary's hardcore it still comes back by itself at the next log-in or room change, and this lets them bring it back sooner. With "Farm visors" switched off there's no 🌀.

- **Companion 0.87.0 + Conditioning 2.6.0: a visor's pet name and trigger, it comes back on its own, an afterimage, and it sinks with them** Laynie, Nov 25: "I think the next step is to add a "suggestions" tool for the visor"; from the ideas she picked "1, 5-8, 10, 11, 12" and said "sounds good" to the plan. This is the wearer's half (1, 10, 11, 12); the room's half (5-8: seen from outside, peek inside, herd trance, the crafter loomin') comes next. The crafter picks the name and the sinkin' in the Visor maker; both go into the visor's code. Visors made before keep their very same code.
  - **The pet name**: while the visor's on, their name in the room is the crafter's pet name (the game's own nickname, so the room's told; 20 letters at most). **It's their trigger**: somebody in the room says it (in a line or an emote) and down they go for a minute or two, or deeper if they're under already. At most once every 2 minutes; never from their own lines, the farm's, or anythin' in brackets. When the visor comes off, the name hangs on: an hour after light sessions, a day after deep ones, and after no-holds-barred till the crafter or a keyholder gives their own name back (`/visor name <name>`). Changed back by hand: in the Dispensary's hardcore the visor puts it back; otherwise that ends it. If their owner keeps their name locked, the farm's voice calls them it anyway and it still takes them down.
  - **It comes back on its own**: taken off with its icon, a visor now stays off even after a reload (it used to come back). In the Dispensary's hardcore, the icon only holds it off till they next log in or join a room, then it takes hold again and a session starts straight away. A safeword's off always sticks till the visor's taken off and put back on.
  - **The afterimage**: after it comes off, a faint spiral at the edges of the ordinary screen and the odd word of the visor's driftin' by: 15 minutes after light sessions, 2 hours after deep, 6 after no-holds-barred. It lasts through a reload, holds still with the calm look, and the trance card lists it (and the name) with the time left.
  - **It sinks with them** (if the crafter ticks it): counted in hours worn, per wearer and per visor, kept across wearin's. 1 hour: whispers in the chat. 3: tunnel vision. 6: one more session before it comes off. 10: the name holds twice as long. 20: other voices melt, and the afterimage lasts twice as long. 40: one more session again, and bits of the room's own words get fed into the dark. They're told each time it sinks. The crafter or a keyholder starts it over with `/visor reset <name>`.
  - **The safeword** ends all of it at once: the visor lets go, their own name comes back, the afterimage goes.

- **Companion 0.86.0 + Conditioning 2.5.0: visor spirals, chat effects, lines to write, HSC-style effects, and a close-up of the face** Laynie, Nov 25: "I would love more visual effects, especially spirals, and maybe stuff in chat"; then "i love those, so yes please. More effects too, and maybe some different ones similar to HSC?"; then "yes, the close up effect of the face would be nice too if possible". Everything below is picked by the crafter in the Visor maker and goes into the visor's code, so it's locked in like the rest. Visors made before keep their very same code and look.
  - **Spirals**: classic, pinwheel, tight, tunnel (rings driftin' out), two turnin' against each other, hearts, paw prints or bells along a spiral, the visor's words written along it, or none. Turnin' slow (a turn every 14 seconds at the fastest), slower, or still; behind the body, over everything faint, or both; how strong (20-70%); a breathin' zoom and colours driftin', each on or off.
  - **In the chat**: whispers of the visor's words between lines, other people's voices meltin' (words lost, or turned into the visor's words), other voices fadin' a while after they're said, and a spiral turnin' faint over the chat. Never the farm's lines, never the crafter's, and never an out-of-character line.
  - **Lines to write**: the crafter gives a line and how many times (3, 5, 10 or 20). The wearer types it in the chat; a line that matches is counted and never goes to the room. The crafter can make the lines count toward the hardcore lock, so it won't come off till they're written too.
  - **More, HSC-style but the farm's own and all slow** (nothin' flashes; no climax flash): room voices floatin' across the dark, the farm's voice bobbin' over the head, breath puffs from the mouth, tunnel vision closin' in as the session goes deeper, a pink bloom now and then, a meltin' warp through the pictures, ripples, a watchin' crowd of farm silhouettes, the pictures turned into a slow kaleidoscope, risin' hearts, an aura round the body, a trance meter, **a close-up of the face** in a soft circle in the corner, and **the view comin' in close to the face** and back out, slow (closer the deeper they go). The calm look slows it all down and holds the spiral still.
  - **The body's drawn right now**: BC's character pictures are taller than the visor thought (700 over the body for hair and ears, 150 under the feet), so the body came out squashed, small and low. It's cut whole now, ears to feet.

- **Companion 0.85.1 + Conditioning 2.4.1: a visor takes any picture link** Laynie, Nov 25: "in game, the visor created with image links doesnt seem to work." Her links were Google image links (no .jpg on the end), and the visor only took links that ended in .png, .jpg, .gif or .webp, so it quietly left them all out and showed its own patterns. Now any https link to a picture counts (never http, never one that's plainly a web page, a script or a video, and never one with spaces, quotes or brackets; it's only ever shown as a picture). The Visor maker says how many links went in and why any were left out, and mentions when they're Google's little search thumbnails (blurry full screen). A visor whose pictures all fail to load shows the farm's own patterns instead of nothin'. The studio's picture sets take the same links.

- **Companion 0.85.0 + Conditioning 2.4.0: a much bigger trance library, praise and degrade tones, and the farm's own visor pictures** Laynie, Nov 25: "bulk up the sessions content and hypno scripts, choose some pictures, and sexy ones, per species and gender"; "any species and gender", and ?degrade / ?praise followed: "yes".
  - **The starter library, bulked up** (shared/trance-more.js, about 90 new blocks; 126 in all): new inductions (The Glow, Countin' Down in the Straw, Head to Tail, The Milker's Rhythm), deepeners (The Cellar Stairs, The Warm Trough, Pasture Sunset, Up and Down Again, Deeper Every Time), convincers, suggestions for anybody (The Ache, Held on the Edge, Belongs to the Farm, Under Glass…), a pack for every kind of stock (cows and bulls, ponies and horses, pups, dogs, wolves and foxes, kitties, pigs, bunnies, goats, sheep, deer, rats, dragons, goblins) with lines for each body and gender, more triggers (Drop for the Farm, Present, Milk Letdown, Walk On, Who's a Good One, Here Kitty, Sooey), compulsions, hang-ons, wakes, slips and nudges, and eight new sessions, seven of them for visors (Under the Glow, Udder Worship, Stud Service, Pony Trainin', Good Pup, Kitty in Heat, Mud and Trough; Deep Barn Cellar for the farm). Not one clinical word in it (a test holds it to that).
  - **Tone**: a block can say `tone: praise` (only for somebody with ?praise on) or `tone: degrade` (only ?degrade on); everything else reaches anybody. Sessions, slips, nudges and visors all follow it.
  - **The farm's own visor pictures** (`public/visor/pictures/`, 41 originals drawn for the farm, no characters): slow spirals and rings, a slowly turning spiral GIF, mantra posters (GOOD COW, BREED, EMPTY, GOOD PUP, KITTY…), species prints (cow spots, paws, hooves, horseshoes, bells, carrots, snouts), and neon pin-up silhouettes for cows, ponies, pups, kitties, pigs and bunnies, each a girl, a guy and somebody in between. 21 ready-made picture sets (each kind: girls, boys, everyone; plus The Farm's Spirals, Empty Heads, The Breeding Barn) in the farm's public visor list, and in the studio's Visor list.
  - **The studio's picture picker**: drop pictures in `public/visor/pictures/` in the project folder; the Visor list panel shows them as thumbnails, and a click adds one to the set you're editing. Publish for visors sends them to GitHub with the rest. (GitHub doesn't allow pornographic pictures, and no character pictures go there; explicit ones belong on an adult image host, added to a set as links.)
  - **Visors fit their wearer**: the farm tells each Companion their kind, body and tones (kept for off the farm), so a visor's session picks blocks for their kind and tone and only the lines that fit their body.

- **Bot 0.103.0 + Companion 0.84.0 + Dispensary 0.29.0 + Conditioning 2.3.0: the farm's visor: the room goes dark, one body, the crafter's pictures and words, sessions from the farm's public list, a small icon to take it off (locked in hardcore till its sessions are done)**
- **Bot 0.103.0 + Companion 0.84.0 + Conditioning 2.3.0 + Dispensary 0.29.0: the farm's visor** Laynie, Nov 25: "For the hypnotic visor items in bc and echoes, what if we built on a subliminal set of effects… renders nothing else map wise, in combination with our session and binaurals beats? with a onscreen button to turn the visor off… for hardcore mode players, is locked until a set number of sessions is finished"; pictures and GIFs "being links, as long as it points at the pictures address"; off the farm too; any visor; "the keyholders and crafter" open it early; it fills the pull and counts toward tiers; sessions "from a list I make publically accessible"; "once a visor is built, its settings should be locked in unless the crafter changes them"; "The button needs to be a small icon".
  - **Wearin' one**: any worn item whose crafted description says `farm visor: #code` (BC's Hypnotic, Interactive or Futuristic Visor, Echoes' visor, anything). The room's half of the screen goes dark: no map, nobody else, just one body (the crafter's, or the wearer's own, as the crafter picked), the crafter's pictures see-through underneath, subliminal words fadin' in and out (never a strobe: one at most every 2 seconds, under a second each, never bright; "Calm trance look" makes them drift), and the farm's trance on top (LSCG's own in the farm's name if they let the farm in, its beats if they turned them on). Sessions from the farm's public list play one after another while it's on; the chat stays. A small 👁 icon in the corner takes it off.
  - **Hardcore** (the Dispensary's): the icon shows 🔒 till the visor's number of full sessions are done. The crafter or the wearer's keyholders open it early with `/visor unlock <name>` in their own Companion. The safeword always ends it (the farm's, LSCG's or the game's own). A fresh wearin' starts the count again.
  - **On the farm**, a finished visor session counts toward their conditioning tiers (never past the depth they allow) and feeds the farm's pull. Visor sessions are trance only: induction, deepeners, convincers, suggestions and the wake (no triggers, compulsions or hang-ons; those are the farm's to plant).
  - **The Visor maker** (Companion › Settings › 🌀 Visor maker): pick sessions from the farm's list, how many before a hardcore wearer can take it off, whose body, the words, the pictures (one of the farm's sets, or their own links), how strong. It gives a line for the item's description. The code in it is a fingerprint of exactly those settings (the picture links too), so a visor stays as it was built: change anything and it's a new code, and only the crafter can change their own craft's description. Companions hand a tuning to each other in the room (the code proves it's the right one), and the farm keeps a copy, so it works anywhere once it's been seen.
  - **The farm's public list** (the trance studio): tick "for visors" on the sessions crafters may pick; keep picture sets (a name and links straight to pictures) in the new Visor list panel; **Publish for visors** writes them to `public/visor/` and sends just those files to GitHub, where every Companion reads them. Till the first publish, visors play the starter library's sessions.
  - **Their own switch**: "Farm visors" in the Companion's trance settings (on unless switched off). Off, a farm visor's just a visor.

- **Bot 0.102.0 + Companion 0.83.0 + Conditioning 2.2.0: the farm's trance, part 3: compulsions, hang-ons, and the deeper the more permanent** Laynie, Nov 24-25: compulsions "would be amazing", with "a small, random chance of them triggering"; hang-ons wanted; "The deeper they go, the more permanent some of it becomes"; permanent ones removed by the "handler or proprieter"; then "part three then".
  - **Compulsions**: a session plants them like triggers, but no word sets them off. Somethin' happenin' does: comin' up out of a session, their herd leader walkin' in or talkin', a milkin' stall takin' them, bein' near full, a feeder feedin' them, a pat on the head, hands on their tits, cummin'. A small chance each time (10% unless the block says, at most 50%), never the same one twice in ten minutes, never while a trap, a scene or ?quiet has them, a session's runnin', or they're away from the keys. Their Companion reaches in like a trigger: give in or fight it. They're made of the same steps as a trigger (LSCG's and the farm's own).
  - **Hang-ons**: they stay a while after the session: moo talk (their sound slips out between their words), no I just this animal, every no comes out yes, kept at the edge, kept needy, down low (their own pick, kneelin' or all fours), hands off themselves, only the farm's voice (hearin' narrowed), names gone fuzzy, drawn to their herd leader's side. They're told what and for how long; the Companion's trance card lists what's holdin' on; out-of-character lines, commands, whispers and emotes are never touched; the safeword ends them all.
  - **The deeper, the more permanent**: from a Fun session, what's planted fades in a day and a compulsion fires once; from Deep, a week, renewed each time it lands; from No human left, set for good, and only their herd leader or a proprietor takes it out (?planted remove). Hang-ons last at most 20 minutes from Fun, an hour from Deep, four hours from No human left. Faded triggers come out of their LSCG the next time they're under in the farm's name, and so do ones a herd leader takes out. ?hypno off rests all of it.
  - **The trance library and the studio's editor**: two more kinds, compulsion (when, chance, does) and hangon (effect, lasts). The starter library has seven compulsions and seven hang-ons, and its sessions use them (Fun: a hang-on; Deep: a compulsion and a hang-on; No human left: two of each). Times can be in hours now (1h, 1h30m), and ?planted and the editor call the farm's own steps by name.

    - **In and out of the room:** bring the room's map in by pasting `/mapcopy` (or from the bot's backup), and take one out with **Copy for /mappaste**.
    - **The map doc** (`docs/farm-map-decoded.txt`) is written from the studio. Traps are never in it, since that page is public; the studio's server refuses one that has a trap in it.
  - **Swapping a layout in for an event, two steps:** paste its map with `/mappaste`, then **`?layout <name>`** so the farm girl takes its spots and zones from the studio. **`?layout back`** puts the old ones back, and **`?layout`** says which is in and lists the rest. Proprietors only. A spot that didn't move keeps who set it and when.
  - **Safety:** the studio only listens to the bot's PC. No website can save a layout or read the bot's backup, and only the bot's own game tab can read a layout (for `?layout`). The release tool now also refuses to commit anything from `studio/maps/`.
  - **Built the new way** (docs/DIRECTION.md): TypeScript and Preact, type-checked by the tests. The first piece of the new setup, and it doesn't touch the live farm.
- **Bot 0.71.0 + Breeding 1.3.0: every kind births its own way, and the midwives get real work.** Laynie, Nov 10: "revisit pregnancy and Midwife mechanics. Midwifes need a way to check progress, speed labor along, variable messages per species". Her picks: midwives are "staff and any trusted", checkups "on by default", a stuck one is fine "as long as its not a bad thing".
  - **Every kind its own way** (bot, 10y2b-birthing.js). Before, everybody moo'd through labour and farrowed like a sow. Now a cow calves in the calvin' pen and licks her calf clean, a mare foals front hooves first, a bitch whelps and tears each sac open, a bunny pulls her own fur to line a kindlin' nest, a cat queens in the loft purrin', wolves and foxes den, goats kid, ewes lamb, rats shred straw, goblins steal blankets, and a dragon scorches a hollow. Their own young (calf, foal, pups, kits, hatchlings…) and their own sounds, and the farm's shared lines fill in the same words. A single reads like one ("a calf, a little female"), a litter like a litter ("4 pups, 2 male and 2 female").
  - **Labour takes as long as it takes:** 45 minutes, plus 15 for a first litter, plus 3 for every young past the first (30 at most), less 10 once they've birthed twice. It moves through early squeezes, harder ones, then pushin'.
  - **A stuck one, now and then,** past the middle of labour (likelier in a litter): one comes turned crossways and everything waits. A midwife turns it; nobody comes in 10 minutes and the farm girl does it herself. The time stuck is added back, so it's only ever a wait.
  - **Midwives** (breeding add-on, midwife.js): staff, plus anybody a herdmaster or proprietor trusts with **?midwives add <who>** (?midwives lists them, ?midwives remove <who>).
    - **?checkup <who>**, standin' right beside them: how far along, how long to go, how they're carryin', and a guess at how many that sharpens as they go (too soon → at least two → three, maybe four → exact). The midwife hears it privately, the room sees hands on the belly, and the one carryin' is told what was found. A staff point for each, once a day. On for everybody; **?checkup off** keeps hands off you. **?checkup** on your own gives a rougher feel.
    - **?midwife <who>** in labour: the first time takes the job; every time after, once every 3 minutes (any midwife), coaches, rubs, holds or helps 'em push, and takes 5 minutes off. 45 minutes alone comes down to about 20 with somebody workin' at it. A stuck one: it turns it. Just after the birth (an hour): it tends them, and their milk stays fresh a day longer. Before labour: a checkup.
    - **?induce <who>** once they're nestin': they're asked first, and on a yes their water breaks with the one who brought it on as their midwife.
    - **The call:** when labour starts, staff on duty and trusted midwives on the farm are told where; a stuck one calls them again.
    - **The pay:** 2 staff points for the midwife, one more for each help (four at most) and one for turnin' a stuck one. Trusted midwives who aren't staff get ribbons. Two helps or more and the one who birthed gets a ribbon for a smooth birthin'. The birth's keepsake names the midwife.
    - **🩺 Expectin' on the farm:** a card for staff (in the Staff panel) and trusted midwives: who's carryin', how far, when they're due, who's in labour and whether one's stuck, when each was last checked, with buttons.
- **Chat records kept off GitHub.** Three of the farm's chat records (backup/logs) went out with the Nov 7 release, because the ignore list only named "Backups/". They're gone from the files, the whole backup folder and every record-*.txt are ignored, and `npm run release` now refuses to commit anything that looks private.
- **Companion 0.54.4: the farm girl's tab lets go of folks who left.** Laynie, Nov 7, after another crash at 14:49: "it was a mem crash", and "just a cache clear type thing, right".
  - **What it was:** when somebody leaves the room, the game takes them off the room's list but keeps them loaded, drawing and all, till *you* leave the room. Players hop rooms, so theirs clears all the time. The bot never leaves the farm, so everybody who ever came by stayed loaded till her tab died. The first 0.54.3 memory log showed 28 characters loaded with 8 in the room, holding 133 MB of drawings.
  - **Now, on the bot's tab only,** anybody gone from the room for 10 minutes is let go, the game's own way. Come back, and the game loads them fresh like any newcomer. Never the bot herself, anybody still in the room, anybody whose profile is open, or the game's own characters, and never while the bot's out of the room. The farm keeps people in its ledger, not that list, so nothin' it does changes.
  - **/farmmem says how many loaded characters have left the room,** and how many were let go.
  - (Also: the earlier "under 1%" for the farm's own work counted only the farm's own lines. Counting the game work it sets off, it's about 4% of the bot's tab.)
- **Companion 0.54.3: the farm girl's screen naps, and old pictures get let go again.** Laynie, Nov 7: "the memory used by the page is skyrocketing and crashing it sometimes", on the bot's tab only, with the farm itself under 1% of the work in every recording.
  - **What it was:** the game's newest version (R133) changed how it finds a picture to draw. Before, a quick look in a list. Now, every picture on every frame gets a time stamp, a "just in case" error note and a loading job, all thrown away straight after. It also keeps every picture unpacked, in two places (the screen's and the graphics card's), and only clears out the ones unused for half an hour, once an hour. Switching the bot's mods to stable and turning the extras off changed nothing in the recordings, so it wasn't them.
  - **The screen nap (the bot's own tab only):** after 2 minutes with nobody touching the bot's tab, the map isn't drawn, just a plain "nappin'" card. A mouse move, click, wheel or key wakes it at once. The bot hears, answers, milks and moves the same either way. Settings → Advanced → "Let the farm girl's screen nap" switches it off.
  - **The picture trim works on the new game again.** It had stepped aside thinking the game's new cache cleaned up after itself. Now on everybody's tab, past 380 MB of pictures, the ones nobody's drawn for 5 minutes go, oldest first, down to 260 MB. On the bot's tab, every picture unused for 5 minutes goes, every minute. Anything let go loads again from the browser's own copy when it's wanted.
  - **/farmmem counts what it missed:** the screen's pictures, every character the game has loaded (and their drawings), and how often the bot's screen has napped.
- **Bot 0.70.6 + Companion 0.54.2: the bot's tab keeps its chat log short.** Laynie, Nov 7: "as soon as i turn admin powers on the tabs ram usage shoots through the roof", then "even with the admin powers off, the ram usage is spiking way up again", only on the bot's tab.
  - **What it was:** the game never trims its chat log by itself (only its Cut button does), and the bot's tab runs all day with thousands of lines an hour, so every line ever said stayed on the page. The admin power (whole-map sight and hearing) only made it climb faster, by showin' every line on the map. /farmmem's "page" number stayed flat because that's the scripts' memory, not the page's.
  - **Now the bot keeps the last 500 lines** on its own tab (the oldest go, the room's divider stays), checked every 20 seconds. Only the bot's account: players' chat logs are left alone. The bot hears everything from the game's connection, never from this log, so nothin' it does changes.
  - **/farmmem now shows the chat log's lines and the page's elements,** so the page's own weight shows next time.
  - The admin power isn't needed on the bot's tab: the bot hears every message anywhere on the map either way, and never asks the game who can see or hear whom.
  - tests/chatlog-trim.test.cjs: a 1,200-line log cut to 500, the room's divider and the newest lines kept, a short log left alone.
- **Bot 0.70.5 + Companion 0.54.1: a game that won't let the farm move somebody, and pinnin' is off till switched on.** From the Nov 7 watch report (Laynie: "It says Exe is trying to summon alexia and i, seems like some kind of bug, because exe isnt staff").
  - **What it was:** not Exe summonin' anybody. The bot was tryin' to put Exe back where somethin' held them (Laynie: "the script half loaded on Exe's end, and i think Alexia and I tried to milk them before they were fully in, so the bot got confused on wheter to pin them"). Exe's game won't let the bot move them, and answered every try with "A teleportation attempt has been cancelled because not allowed", out loud in the room under Exe's name. The bot tried again every few seconds (21 times in the 4 minutes recorded).
  - **Now, when somebody's game says no to a teleport the bot just sent,** it stops tryin' to move them for half an hour (or till they come back into the room). Whatever was holdin' them lets them be, they're told once, privately, the stocks stop whisperin' "back in the stocks you go", the pen stops tellin' the room it walked them back, and staff who ?summon them are told why. A "no" the bot didn't just ask for is ignored.
  - **A pin is never taken off the map.** A game still loadin' somebody in has them at -1,-1; the pin waits till they're placed, an old pin sittin' off the map moves to where they stand, and the bot never sends anybody to a spot off the map.
  - **?pinme: "Can be pinned", off till they switch it on** (Laynie: "we can add a 'can be pinned toggle'. some players seem really frustrated with the mechanic", and off by default, like the traps). Too full or too big to move, stuck where you stand till you're milked down, drained or given a reducer shot, twenty minutes at most, safeword frees you. Off, they just waddle: the cumflation line says "so swollen they can barely waddle" instead of "stuck right where they are". It's in ⚙ Settings › Farm switches › 🥛 Milk, and ?pinme on|off from chat. Anybody pinned now is let go on the next tick unless they switch it on.
  - tests/teleport-refused.test.cjs covers all of it, from a no to the stocks, the summon, the half hour, a pin off the map, and ?pinme.
- **Bot 0.70.4 and every part of the farm girl: the parts' manifests, a starter part, and one line checker (Phases 4 and 5 of the plan).** Laynie, Nov 4: "phase 4 and 5 sound innocent enough, go ahead". Nothin' changes in play except four lines worded better. Install the parts' updates along with the bot (Tampermonkey picks them up).
  - **Every part tells the bot its real version now.** Each one had said 1.0.0 (or close) for months while the release bumped its addon.json: glory stalls was really 1.10.1, barn life 1.4.1. The build now puts the addon.json version into the script, so the Dashboard's 🧩 Bot parts tab shows the right numbers, and they can't drift again.
  - **A starter part:** `addons/_template` is a whole, workin' part (a command, a game event, a Companion card, lines that fit) with a README listin' everything a part can hand the bot: its commands, the game events it can hear, its cards, rates, lines and vetoes. Copy the folder to make a new one.
  - **tests/bot-parts.test.cjs:** every part's addon.json is whole, each part plugs in under its folder's name with its addon.json's version, none takes a word the bot or another part has, and the starter builds, plugs in, answers ?wave and shows its card.
  - **One line checker, on every release (tests/lines.test.cjs):** it reads every pool of lines twice, from the source (the bot's parts and every part, not the private one) and from the pools the runnin' bot holds (so lines added later are read too): about 17,000 lines. Every line must have no clinical word, no {tag} that would show, no tag the bot doesn't know, no odd quote mark, no "the the", no "undefined", and not be in its own pool twice. The line reader is shared with tools/pool-audit.mjs, which still lists the softer things (a body word with no tag, a "her" that may be the farm girl) for somebody to read.
  - **What it caught, reworded the farm's way (Laynie read each one):** two filthy trap lines ("for one orgasm right now" is now "for the chance to cum right now, and it still wouldn't let them"; "can't finish one orgasm" is now "can't finish cummin'"), "very aroused" is "so worked up" in a conditioning line, and "a rush of fluid" is "a warm wet rush" at the farrowin'.
  - **"Orgasm" isn't on the clinical list.** The parasite's line was reworded to "the whole climb" and put straight back: Laynie, "misleading and super vague. it was honestly better with the word orgasm". A plain, exact word beats a vague one.
  - **The lone wolf gives three loads now, not two** (Laynie: "can always boost the base amount of loads, so it always gaurentees there is enough"). Each is as big as before, and his two lines that count them say "Three knots, an hour each" and "He ties %n three times". The wolf den never gives fewer than three.
  - **Four shaky tests fixed for good** (they only passed on a second try): the tickle-rack bet now waits for the rack to open its book; the grooming line and a trap handin' somebody on get a little longer on a busy machine; and the dairy stall's line is matched against every line the dairy part has (its more-lines file too), not a few words.
  - **Left out on purpose:** movin' the lines into separate data files, and loadin' the seasons only in their season. The seasons are about a tenth of the bot's script, the page crashes were the game's pictures not the script, and loadin' them later would mean the bot fetchin' from GitHub while it runs: one more way to break, for little gain. And a part's ?commands stay in their own list rather than the command book, so a private part stays invisible to everybody else; they keep the book's rules all the same.
- **Bot 0.70.3 + Companion 0.54.0: the Staff panel and the Dashboard tidied too (Phase 3 of the plan, second half).** Laynie, Nov 4: "tidying like that would be good across all panels". Same look as the Livestock panel: four tabs across the top, and when a panel has more, the rest are under ⋯ at the end of the strip. The 🧸 Nursery keeps its own look.
  - **Staff:** 🗂 Office · 📝 Queue · 🐑 Herd · 📜 Contracts, then under ⋯ 🏚 Barn · ⏱ Shift · 🗺 Zones · 📒 Log (221397 only, as before) and the farm extras.
    - The Office opens on **Look somebody up**: type a name, then Record, Milk, Drain, Inspect, Vet or Quota (or type the whole thing, like "vet Bessie", and press Enter). Safeword calls stay on top.
    - **The Herd is one line each:** name, where, how full, 🔥 for heat, 🍼 for carryin'. Tap one for its buttons. A red dot means they need milkin'.
    - **Your duty and pasture** moved to the top of ⏱ Shift (the old 🧑‍🌾 Me tab).
    - **On call and the ECHS card** moved to ⚙ Settings › 🧑‍🌾 Staff tools (staff only). The guides are in ⚙ Settings › Guides.
    - **Zones stayed on the panel** (not in Settings like the mockup had it): drawin' a zone means clickin' the map, and the game's settings screen covers the map.
  - **Dashboard (proprietors only, same as now):** 📜 BC+ contracts · 👗 Outfits · 🗃 Records · 💡 Suggestions, and 🧩 **Bot parts** under ⋯ (it was "Other addons").
    - **Bot parts lists every part of the farm girl** with its version, on or off, and any errors. Tap one for its guide or to switch it off.
    - **Laynie's own part shows only on 221397's Dashboard**, with a 🔒 and no buttons. The bot leaves it out of every other panel.
    - **🏅 Feats this week:** who earned which (the gold-star ones stay in the nursery).
    - BC+ and BCX summoning checks are at the bottom. The old "Comin' soon" card is gone: all of it came.
  - The Guest panel: Welcome · The farm · Rules · Inbox, with Guides under ⋯.
  - Bot: proprietors' panels carry the bot parts and the week's feats.
  - Tests: the Companion test follows the new Staff and Dashboard tabs; the lookout test checks the private part is listed for its owner only and that the other proprietor can't switch it off by name. A shaky check in oct26b (a line about Moo arrivin' late, during Rex's turn) now only looks at lines about Rex.
- **Bot 0.70.2 + Companion 0.53.0: the tidy Livestock panel and the settings pages (Phase 3 of the plan, first half).** Laynie, Nov 4, on the mockups: "thats so much cleaner, and i like the aesthetic of the livestock panels", and "tidying like that would be good across all panels". Nothing was taken away: it moved, or it folds into a row you tap.
  - **Four tabs that fit the width:** 🐄 Me · 🥛 Body · 🪤 Play · 🎀 Ribbons (it was ten).
  - **🐄 Me is one card and some rows.** The card has your name and ribbons, a line with your kind, your herd and how long your contract has left, chips for what's on you right now, and small meters: milk, quota, seed, holding, and barn life's food, water and grooming. Under it, one line each for the inbox (a red dot when there's somethin' new), what's got you, carryin', body, today's marks, kept, roam, your next feat and the farm extras. Tap one and it opens right there; tap it again and it folds. Only what's happenin' shows: no kept, no kept row.
  - **Quick buttons on Me:** 🚿 Wash, 💉 Shots, 🪣 Drain (yourself, nobody needed), and More ▾ for stats, measure, record, pedigree, keys, dress me and the quiet hour.
  - **🥛 Body:** milk (udder, quota, the pump you're on, the stall dial), breedin', holdin' with Drain and Wash, and seed. Not makin' seed? It points you to the Futa switch.
  - **🪤 Play:** your trap card with Struggle and What's out there (the rest under More ▾), then rows for what's on you, kinds of trap, potions in you, a dare, the corral, the nest, what you've taken, keepsakes and your shots; proprietors get their trap and Seasons rows here too.
  - **🎀 Ribbons:** your ribbons and the vat, then a row for where they came from, each shelf of the store, your feats and titles.
  - **⚙ Settings, in the header,** opens Preferences → Extensions → Farmhand Companion, now in eight pages: 🐄 Farm switches · 🪞 Body and look · ⛓ Gear and marks · 💬 Messages · 🔒 Privacy and record · 📖 Guides · 🧰 Advanced · ℹ About, with a **Find a setting** box that looks through all of them. "How the farm talks to you" is on Messages. Where the game has no settings page, the same pages open inside the panel.
  - **The record switch says exactly what Laynie asked:** "Send a record of what I see: This option sends a copy of what the companion receives and sends for bug squashing. Completely voluntary participation." Only 221397 can read the logs, the same as before.
  - **The Futa switch** says what it's for now: a cock of your own, to be a stud for a while and flip it back.
  - **Quieter header:** small ⚙ ⛶ 🆘 ✕ icons; only 🆘 keeps its red edge. Drag, resize and full screen work as before.
  - **Fixed:** anybody with only one panel saw the word "false" under the header (since v0.2.0). And a tab you switch to now starts at its top instead of where the last one was scrolled.
  - The Guest panel's Settings tab is gone (the ⚙ has it all). Staff, the Dashboard and the Guest panel get the same tidyin' next; the 🧸 Nursery stays as it is.
  - Bot: each panel now carries the contract you're under and when it ends, for the Me card.
  - tests/tidy-panel.test.cjs: the four tabs, the card, rows that open and fold, the quick buttons, every old switch found on a settings page, her exact record wording, Find a setting, and no switch for a proprietor's record. The Companion, store and Oct 27 tests follow the new layout; the buttons audit presses ?myshots, a self ?drain and ?quiet.
- **No new versions: the bot's parts sorted into folders (Phase 2 of the plan).** Laynie, Nov 4: no cloud threads editing bot files, so go ahead. Nothing to install: the built bot comes out byte for byte the same.
  - **185 files, 13 folders by topic** in `bot/src/parts/`: core, office, farm, looks, voice, ribbons, parasites, nursery, seasons, and traps (with traps/lines, traps/more and traps/ends). Each folder has a README saying what every file in it holds; the one at the top says which folder has what.
  - **The names didn't change**, only the folders. The build joins the parts by file name whatever folder they're in, and stops if two files ever share a name. git kept each file's history through the move.
  - tools/who-audit.mjs and the command-book test read the new folders.
  - **Two more shaky tests fixed for good:** keepers (fed by hand where they sit) and udders (the let-down after cummin' in the stall) looked for a few words some newer lines don't use. They now take any line from their own pool.
- **Bot 0.70.1: the command book (Phase 1 of the plan).** Laynie, Nov 4: "we are good to proceed". Under the hood only: every command answers exactly as before. No Companion update needed.
  - **One place for every ?command.** `bot/src/parts/01b-commands.js` writes each command down once: its words, who may use it (staff or everybody), whether the answer stays private when asked in the room, whether it never waits its turn (safety), whether it takes free text, whether it works mid-application, and whether staff lookups land in the Office tab. Before, that was seven lists kept by hand in three files.
  - **Parts answer their own commands.** The store, potions and dares, the bench, traps, shots and kryptonite, feats, the nursery, services, parasites and symbiotes, messes, your own outfit, the brood, bets, corruption, cursed suits, keepers, udders, the seasons, trick-or-treat, stockings, the egg hunt, trap ratings, renewals and the suggestion box each write their commands into the book next to the code that runs them. 97 hand-off lines left the big switch in 15-commands.js.
  - **Add a command:** one `addCommand("word alias", { topic, staff, private, run })` line in the part that handles it. A word written down twice is caught by the tests.
  - tests/command-book.test.cjs checks the book gives back exactly the old lists (176 for everybody, 84 for staff, the private ones, safety and the rest), that every command has somebody answering it and only once, and that ?feats, ?store, a staff command, a near miss and ?watersport answer as before.
  - `npm test` now names the files that only passed on a second, solo run, so they can be fixed for good. The first ones it named are fixed:
    - **The bait and bet checks** compared the whole purse, and a feat paid in the same few seconds threw it off by one. They now look for the payment itself in the ribbon log.
    - **Yule's ivy** had the same stale end words as Litha and Mabon.
    - **The thing in the water** has grown more beats, so the egg count now waits for the scene to finish.
    - **The auto-injector check** allows for the heartbeat dosing a second time inside its wait.
- **Bot 0.70.0 + Companion 0.52.0: feats and perks.** Laynie, Nov 3–4: tracks by category whose perks stack as the tiers climb, credit for the past, Trap Magnet as it is, a Gold Star track for littles, and an egg track "at a penalty to how much they earn ribbon wise. cant have them exploiting the tentacle traps". (bot/src/parts/10us-feats.js)
  - **Ten tracks, five tiers each.** A new tier is a feat: announced in the farm's voice, and the tier's number in ribbons (1 to 5). Every perk grows with its track on its own (no slots); `?perks off <perk>` switches one off, `?perks on` brings it back.
    - 🥛 **Dairy** (10, 50, 150, 400, 1000 L milked) → **Heavy Udders**: +5% milk capacity a tier, and a grade higher from III.
    - 🐂 **Breeding stock** (1, 4, 10, 25, 60 L of seed taken) → **Breeder's Belly**: +4% fertility and +100 mL of room a tier.
    - 🍼 **Motherhood** (1, 3, 6, 12, 25 litters) → **Good Mother**: pregnancy 5% shorter a tier, and twins far likelier at V.
    - 🥚 **Egg layer** (10, 50, 150, 400, 1000 eggs) → **Prolific Layer**: clutches 10% bigger and 8% quicker a tier, but eggs fetch 15% fewer ribbons a tier at market and at a hatchin' (down to a quarter).
    - 💦 **Stud** (10, 50, 150, 400, 1000 covers) → **Prize Stud**: loads +5% a tier, likelier to catch at IV and V.
    - 🪤 **Trapped** (10, 40, 100, 250, 600 traps) → **Trap Magnet**: traps reset and find you 10% sooner a tier, and from III a trap sometimes pays a ribbon.
    - 📋 **Hard worker** (best quota streak 3, 7, 14, 30, 60 days) → **Reliable**: a free quota grace a week at II (two at IV), a ribbon more for milk a day at V.
    - 🎀 **Farm favourite** (25, 100, 250, 600, 1500 ribbons earned) → **Favourite**: 2% off at the store a tier (on things over a ribbon).
    - 🗝️ **Mischief** (1, 5, 15, 40, 100: kryptonite found, bets won, parasites coaxed) → **Sharp Eyes**: bets pay 2.1 to 2.5 times, and the kryptonite rumours show more letters.
    - ⭐ **Gold stars** (littles only: 5, 20, 50, 120, 300 nursery stars) → **Gold Star Little**: an extra star a day at II (two at IV), stars come easier from III. Private like the rest of the nursery: never announced to the room, never shown to anybody outside it.
  - **The past counts.** The first time the farm looks, every tier somebody's totals already earn is theirs, in one quiet message (no ribbons for the back-pay). The ribbons-earned count starts from the purse they hold.
  - `?feats` · `?feats <who>` · `?perks`. The Companion has a 🏅 **Feats** tab with a bar for every track and a switch for every perk. The eleven titles (Cream Queen and the rest) show alongside.
  - **Prices show the perk.** `?store`, the panel's Store tab and the roam-pass button all show what that player actually pays, Favourite discount included. Bounties stay at their full price.
  - **Tests that failed now and then, fixed.** Litha's "heat ruled out", Mabon's cider press and Easter's "eggs ruled out" checked for words from a trap's first three end lines, but more end lines came later (10z6e-trap-ends-5.js), so a newer one drawn at random failed the check. They now accept any line from that trap's own ending. Trap tests also wait for the send queue to empty before they look.
  - **`?record` shows a waiting application too.** Laynie, Nov 4: the record should show their application. It already did once they were approved; now staff (and the applicant) also see the one still waiting in the queue, marked ⏳.
- **Bot 0.69.1: the safeword holds kryptonite off for an hour.** Laynie, Nov 3: yes. After a proprietor's safeword, nothin' carryin' their word (a needle, an injector, a piercing or a drip) can drop them again for an hour. No Companion update needed.
- **Bot 0.69.0: the farm reads descriptions properly, and the IV drip never lets up.** Laynie, Oct 31: "need better checking inside the descriptions of items, not just the names on them. the iv drip for instance should be a constant top up". No Companion update needed.
  - **Descriptions in plain words.** Shot words were already read from descriptions, but only as exact phrases, so a description saying it the way people write missed. Now word forms and sentences count, for example:
    - "keeps her lactating", "makes him virile", "more fertile", "arousing", "sedates", "lays eggs in her"
    - Injectors: "injects the wearer with…", "doses her with…"
    - Drips: "an IV line that slowly drips … into her", "a steady drip", "intravenous", "infusion"
    - Triggers: "whenever she's bred", "every time they cum", "while she's milked", "each time she's edged"
    - Roulette: "random doses", "mystery serum"

  - **Ostara (Mar 17 to 23, the spring equinox): 6 traps.** The March hares (two hare-men boxin' over who gets you first), the Green Man out of the hedge (a farmhand in a mask of new leaves; a crown of violets and a dustin' of pollen after), the first sowin' (tied to the plough in the first furrow), the egg-dyein' tubs (every pastel colour, and flowers painted on), the equinox see-saw (sat on the middle post and kept right on the edge), and the thaw-brook nixie (a clutch of her pearly spawn). The warren, the egg posts, the seed shower, the vines and the plough come round more, and a tile named `trap-ostara` (or `trap-spring`) springs only Ostara's. The farm girl wears a spring flower crown, hare's ears and a hare's tail.

  - **`?egghunt`** all Easter week: hunt for the eggs hidden round the farm, once every 20 minutes. A chocolate egg (it works like one of the sweet potions), a soft one that goes all over you (with "Farm can mark my body"), now and then a golden egg worth a ribbon (once a day at most), or a trick: a strange jelly bean, or, with your traps on, an egg that was bait for one of Easter's traps. `?egghunt board`: who's found the most this Easter.
  - **Litha (Jun 18 to 24, the summer solstice): 6 traps.** The midsummer play (the Holly King comes back for the year, as he promised at Yule; the two kings share the prize, and you wear the Oak King's crown after), the fairy ring on Midsummer's Eve (the fae dance you round till dawn), leapin' the midsummer bonfire (whoever catches you keeps you till sunrise), the sun wheel (the real Wheel of Fortune: tied spread and turned with the sun all afternoon), the mead hall (bent over the barrel and licked clean), and Robin Goodfellow's flower (you wake wantin' the first one you see, and you're in heat after, unless heat's in your limits). The hay bales, the honey pot, the corn maze, the windmill, the washin' line and the hot tub come round more. The farm girl wears a crown of midsummer roses.
  - **Mabon (Sep 19 to 25, the autumn equinox, the harvest home): 6 traps.** The last sheaf (bound up in it, and every reaper takes a turn for luck), the corn maiden (woven into a straw corn dolly, hung in the rafters and kept on the edge), treadin' the grapes, the cider press (milked into the cider if you make milk), the harvest crown (paraded on the last wagon and shared out at the supper), and the harvest supper (the feast laid out on you). The scarecrow, the bales, the corn maze, the plough, the windmill, the butter churn and the pie contest come round more. The farm girl wears a crown of poppies and a harvest apron.
  - **Any time, too:** `?seasons start ostara` (or `spring`), `easter`, `litha` (or `midsummer`), `mabon` (or `harvest`), or `?seasons start all` for all seven at once.
