- **Bot 0.54.0 + Companion 0.48.0: on the reins, holdin' somebody's place, welcome home, another contract?, winter and Yule.** Laynie, Oct 27: "I love the features ideas, minus the daily report ... winter pack yes ... soltice and equinox based packs with pagan/wiccan themes would be cool."
  - **Update the bot's own tab to Companion 0.48 too.** The winter costume, the new messes, the reins and the renewal cards need it.
  - **On the reins.** `?reins <who> [minutes]`: a herd leader (or a proprietor) leads one of their own herd at heel on the map. They follow a step behind wherever the leader walks, at their own pace (their Companion walks them; without one, or with "Walk me places" off, I hop them along). An hour unless you say, 5 minutes to 4 hours. While a trap, the stocks, a pen, the bench, the milkin' stall or a knot has them they stay put, and the reins pick up again after. `?reins off [who]`, their safeword, either of them leavin' the room or the time runnin' out lets go. In the Companion: a chip for the one on the reins, a "Let go" card for the leader, and a Reins button on the Herd tab. (`?walk` is still the old lead.)
  - **Holdin' somebody's place.** `?away hold <who> 30d [why]` (staff) pauses somebody's two weeks while they're away on purpose: a trip, exams, a hospital stay (12h, 2w, 30d all work, up to a year). They're told. While it's on they get no notes and never come off the books, and when it's up their clock carries on from where it stopped. `?away hold <who> off` ends it early; `?away` lists who's on hold. The 12-day note now says a hold can be asked for.
  - **Welcome home.** Somebody who got the 12-day note and then steps back into the farm is greeted by name and told their place is kept, and their herd leader hears they're back.
  - **Another contract?** A day before a timed farm contract ends (three hours before, for one that's a day long or less) I ask them if they'd like another, and tell their herd leader. In the room it's a yes/no card in their Companion; away, it's a beep, and I ask again when they come by. `?renew yes` or `?renew no` any time before it ends (changin' your mind is fine). A yes goes to staff and their herd leader, and to the top of the Staff panel's Contracts tab with an **Offer it** button: the same contract, the same length (BC+ only takes an offer in person, so they need to be in the room). It waits there a week after the old one ends. Staff: `?renew` (who's said what), `?renew offer <who>` (herdmasters and the proprietors).
  - **Winter (Dec 1 to Jan 31): 10 traps.** The frozen duck pond (skates froze fast, and every skater takes a turn), the gingerbread man the bakers brought to life (icin' after), mistletoe over the kissin' gate, the yeti down from the hills (wrapped in his fur after), gift-wrapped in a box under the farm's big tree and tagged UNWRAP ME, the giant stockin' by the hearth, the sauna by the pond, the cocoa stand (milked into the pot if you make milk, or the cocoa poured over you), the frost-fae in the hedgerow (kept right at the edge), and the farmyard snowman. The trapper's bearskin rug, the lumberjacks' hot tub, the bakery bench and the kissin' gate come round more, and a tile named `trap-winter` (or `trap-snow`) springs only winter's. The farm girl wraps up in a red winter hat, shawl and fur gloves.
  - **`?stocking`** all winter: once a day, look in your stockin' on the farm's hearth. A sweet that works like one of the sweet potions, a sweet mess (with "Farm can mark my body"), now and then a ribbon, or coal if you've been naughty since last time (a naughty mark, or a naughty or degraded tier), with NAUGHTY LIST written on you.
  - **Yule (Dec 18 to 24, the winter solstice): 6 traps,** inside winter, so both packs spring that week. Tied astride the Yule log as it's hauled in from the woods; the solstice play, where two farmhands crowned the Holly King and the Oak King fight for the year and share the prize (you, and the Holly King's crown to wear after); the Wild Hunt ridin' down out of the storm; wassailin' the orchard; the old barn's ivy, which holds you to the wall and lays its berry pods in you; and the ring of candles through the longest night (red wax). The rutting stag, the lantern and the bearskin rug come round more. The farm girl wears a holly crown and a deep red hooded cloak.
  - **Any time, too.** Like every season, a proprietor can run winter or Yule whenever they like: `?seasons start winter` (or `yule`, or both), or the Seasons card.
  - **New messes the Companion draws:** royal icin' (with sprinkles), cocoa, eggnog, peppermint syrup (candy-striped) and candle wax. `?wash` takes them off like the rest.
  - **Guides:** `?help` lists `?stocking`, and for staff `?reins`, `?away hold` and `?renew`.
- **Bot 0.53.0 + Companion 0.47.0: Halloween week, rate a trap, the trap of the day, kinds of trap, how you're left, the things in the water.** Laynie, Oct 27: "I love the features ideas, minus the daily report ... more trap versions, like the water beast could use alot more versions. halloween pack, but alot more, full of tricks and treats ... aftermath lines, yes."
  - **Update the bot's own tab to Companion 0.47 too.** The farm girl's costume and the new gear pieces only go on with it.
  - **Seasons.** The farm has a calendar now (`?seasons`, or `?calendar`; `?season` stays the Breedin' add-on's breedin' week). While a season's on, about a third of what the plain trap tiles spring comes from its own pack (the ones that fit the place, when some do), a tile named `trap-halloween` springs nothin' else, the farm girl dresses for it, and the room is told when it starts and ends. Out of season its traps never spring, unless a proprietor starts it. Winter and Yule come next, then Ostara, Easter, Litha and Mabon.
  - **Any season, any time.** Laynie, Oct 27: "i would like to have commands to turn on the holiday traps any time, like for special occasions, just because". Proprietors: `?seasons start <name> [how long]` runs a season's traps now, whatever the date: `2h` (the default), `90m`, `3d`, `2w`, or `on` to keep it goin' till you stop it. Several at once (`?seasons start halloween winter 3d`) or every pack (`?seasons start all on`). `?seasons stop` ends the ones you started (`?seasons stop <name>` or `?seasons stop all` switches off the calendar's own too, till its time's over). `?seasons costume on|off` for the farm girl's dressin' up. The Companion has it as buttons too: the **Seasons** card on the Traps tab, and on the Staff view's Barn tab (2 hours, Till I stop it, Stop, All of them). Players with their Seasonal packs switch off still never get them. (`?trap spring` still springs any one trap by hand.)
  - **Halloween week (Oct 24 to Nov 1): 18 traps.** Somebody waitin' in these: the headless horseman at the covered bridge, the vampire countess (drinks half your milk and leaves you BITTEN), the hellhounds behind the burnt chapel (hot knots, and one left in you), the root cellar demon (a heat, a little corruption, little horns for half an hour), the spider queen (webbed, silk-tied, a clutch of her eggs), the ghost in the old milkin' parlour (cold hands, warm hands, glowin' slime), the Samhain fire's masked revellers (antlers to wear after), the hayride ghouls, the pumpkin patch's pickers, and whoever's behind you at the apple barrel. Equipment: the witch's cauldron (a random potion; your limits and potion switch rule), her broom (it keeps you on the edge), the candy apple vat, the taffy puller, the mummy's wraps, the haunted house, the costume trunk (ears and a tail of another kind for an hour) and the treat door. New messes: pumpkin, caramel, ghost slime, cider and the witch's brew.
  - **`?trickortreat`** (or `?tot`) during Halloween: knock on the farm girl's door, once every 20 minutes. A treat is a candy that does somethin' nice (clover, golden, honey, musk, feather, heat mist), a sweet mess, or now and then a ribbon (once a day). A trick is a silly candy (moo, bell, hiccups, needy…), TRICKED written on you, or, with your traps on, a Halloween trap.
  - **The farm girl's costume:** a witch's hat, black cat ears and a black cat tail, put on by her own Companion. Only those pieces, never a stand-in, so if her hat doesn't show it's because the game calls it somethin' else: tell Claude what it's called.
  - **Rate a trap.** When a trap lets you go, the Companion asks how it was: 💖 Loved it · 👍 Fine · 🙅 Not for me. Loved it makes that trap a bit more likely for you. Not for me makes it less likely, and twice makes it a lot less likely (about a quarter as often), but it never stops it altogether (`?traps skip` still does that). Only your own odds change. Without the Companion you're whispered the question the first time each trap has you. `?rate` (what you've rated), `?rate <trap> loved|fine|no`, `?rate ask off`. Staff: `?traps ratings` (most loved, most "not for me", sprung most), also a button on the proprietors' trap card.
  - **The trap of the day.** Each day one of the traps the farm actually has out is picked (never the same two days runnin'): all day it's twice as likely to be the one that springs (the water's twice as hungry when it's the water's), and the first time it catches you that day there's a ribbon in it. The room is told once a day, when somebody with traps on is about. `?traps today`, and the Traps tab shows it.
  - **Kinds of trap, as switches.** Ten, each player's own: furry folk, monsters, goblins and orcs, pregnancy and eggs, milking, messy, restraint, latex, public use and show, and the seasonal packs. Switch one off and not one trap of that kind springs for you, not even one a proprietor springs by hand (a trap that's two kinds is off if either is). On the Companion's Traps and Toggles tabs, or `?traps theme <kind> on|off`, `?traps theme <kind>` to see what's in it.
  - **How you're left.** About two times in five, a few seconds after a trap lets go, the room sees one more line about how it left you, from what really happened: still full and drippin', a clutch in you, milked out, sticky all over, still in its gear, rope marks, a big stud's walk, a heat it started, a foggy head, or the crowd that watched.
  - **The things in the water.** The water's trap isn't always the same thing now. Most often it's the old one, but the farm's water also holds the jelly bloom (its stings leave a heat), the eel-knot (ties in and keeps you longer and fuller), the mother of the pond (rare: twice the clutch, and MOTHER'S BROOD written on you), the hungry one (drinks your milk, and seed with Milk my seed on), the singin' one (puts you under; likelier after dark), the reed-thing (edges you: no cummin' for 8 minutes after), the ink-spitter (black with ink head to toe, `?wash` gets it off), the warm one (slow and tender, and the eggs come a day sooner) and the twins (two at once). The whisper after says which it was, and `?traps tentacle` lists them and which have had you. The old one has about 40 more lines across its scene.
  - **Fixed:** a few older scene lines had children watchin' or throwin' things (at the fair stocks, the windmill, the net, the tomatoes, the doll press, two "kid"s in the studs' mouths). They're milkmaids, stable girls, farmhands and customers now. Nobody in a sexual scene, watchin' or otherwise, is ever a child.
- **Bot 0.52.0 + Companion 0.46.0 + Barn life 1.4.0: less traffic on your connection, the away rule, shrinkin' shots.** Laynie, Oct 27: "looking for what can be merged into the companion to help with lag, and limit how much has to be sent via my connection", plus barn life on the Me tab, shots to shrink things, an update notice for old Companions, and taking people off the books after two weeks away.
  - **Update the bot's own tab to Companion 0.46 first.** Most of the savings only switch on there.
  - **Hellos don't cost a resend any more.** Every Companion says which panel and which store shelf it already holds (the shelf is kept on that computer), so a reload, a re-hello or a ping no longer sends the whole store and panel again. In testing, 10 Companions over 10 minutes went from 322 KB to 45 KB.
  - **One room line for many people.** A line that goes to several Companions at once (a room emote, an announcement) is sent once, and each Companion shows it only if it names them.
  - **Your look goes out in pieces.** When the farm splatters you, writes on you or puts a piece of gear on, only those pieces go to the room (the way the game sends someone tying you up), gathered for under a second. Big changes (an outfit, strip, dress) still go whole, once.
  - **The farm girl's own screen isn't drawn.** On the bot's account the room isn't drawn at all, so its tab doesn't download and draw everyone's items. To sit at its keyboard: Preferences, Extensions, Farmhand Companion, "Draw the room on the farm girl's screen".
  - **Quieter in the background:** sight reports at most every 8 seconds, the friends check every 2 minutes (every minute while a summonable pet is out), the ledger saved at most every 5 seconds (and when the tab closes), keepalives only when nothing else went out, and a whitelist add that never sticks is tried 3 times, then left for an hour.
  - **Old Companions are told.** When someone arrives with an older Companion, a few seconds later they get a note and a beep with how to update (Tampermonkey, "Check for userscript updates", reload). Once a day per version at most.
  - **Barn life on the Me tab.** Food, water, grooming and milk-drunk sit on the Companion's Me tab now, not only in Farm extras.
  - **Shrinkin' shots** (the store's new shelf, a size back down for good): udder, balls, cock, knot (7 ribbons each), ass, pussy, throat (7) and belly (6). At the smallest size the shot refuses and keeps your ribbons.
  - **Away two weeks, off the books.** Livestock, guests and luxury guests who haven't been in the farm room with the bot for 14 days are taken off the books (the same as `?unregister`: paperwork archived, keys pulled, off the whitelist), told by beep or a kept note, and staff are told. A note goes out at 12 days, and steppin' into the room clears it. Never staff, the proprietors, or anybody on a signed permanent contract. The days only count while the bot is in the room, so a bot that's off never costs anybody their place. Everybody's clock started with this release. `?away` (staff) shows who's furthest along.
  - **Plainer words.** Stats, help, the store and room lines say cock, pussy, ass and cum instead of penis, vulva, butt and semen (the old words still work when you type them). "A milk-maker shot", the "milk pump", and "Jar breedin'" on the Companion. `?fair open cock` works.
  - **More variety.** Lines that name the farm girl (or the dairy, kennel, pig, feed, stable or breeding girl) were being skipped for men and nonbinary folk, as if she were a word about *them*, so they saw fewer versions and more repeats. Now everybody gets them, and "the pig girl" never turns into "the pig boy". More lines for every shot (virility had one, now five), more body lines for futas, femboys and pussies, and flat-chest versions in the teat check, slop, feedbag and rubber stall traps. Every trap was sprung on six bodies (female and male cow, femboy bunny, futa and female pony, male goblin): about 1,050 to 1,100 different lines each, none about a part they haven't got, no clinical words, and a fifth fewer repeats than before (the futa body's went from 74 to 44).
  - **Fixed:** a shaky glory stall test; the hello asking for a whole panel again now carries your switches too.
- **Bot 0.51.0 + Companion 0.45.0 + Barn life 1.3.0: less lag, water and grooming, the farm girl's brush, sire bottles.** Laynie, Oct 26.
  - **Less traffic to every Companion.** Panel updates are bunched (many changes in a quarter second go as one), nothing is sent when nothing changed, the store shelf (most of each panel) is sent on its own only when it changes, and after the first full panel only the parts that changed are sent. Older Companions still get the whole panel.
  - **Wrong Barn outfit for Laynie:** whoever drinks it themselves is dressed even with ?ownoutfit on, and when it can't dress them (no Companion in the room, no outfit for that kind) they're told why instead of nothing happening.
  - **The water rinses** (a player's idea): stand on a water spot or trough with a mess on you and after a minute about a third of it rinses off (oldest first, plus one patch of game splatters), again every 3 minutes; the third rinse takes the rest, like ?wash.
  - **The farm girl grooms** anyone on the books as livestock who stops on a tile right next to her (once every 30 min, fitted to their kind: hooves, fleece, dogs, cats, bunnies). Barn life counts it as a grooming. `?grooming off` to opt out.
  - **Her futa grooming traps** (rare, only for people with traps on, only from her brush or a proprietor's `?trap spring`): `groomstand` (cross-tied on the grooming stand and bred), `groomtail` (brushed over the tail rail, then taken in the ass), `groombucket` (scrubbed at the wash bucket, then her cock in their mouth and over their face). She washes them clean first. Goes in the stud book as "the farm girl".
  - **Sire's Claim** (9 ribbons, 2 h, needs a cock): the next person you breed who's already carrying, every litter and clutch in them becomes yours. **One Sire** (9 ribbons, 3 h, drunk by the one carrying): the next load in you, from anyone or anything, becomes the only sire of all of it; on an empty belly, if it takes, no other sire can join. Breeding rights still apply.
  - The farm girl (and the kennel/dairy/milk girl) is no longer called "the farm boy" in lines about a male player.
- **Companion 0.44.0 + Bot 0.50.1: drawing lag.** Laynie, Oct 26: "routing the settings from my pc to all the companions for what to render for the studio is bogging down my connection ... Would building them into the companion instead be feasible".
  - The farm's look for the drawn body is **built into the Companion** now (`shared/farm-look.json`) and updates with it. The bot no longer sends it to anybody (only a Companion older than 0.44 still asks once).
  - To change the look: Save in the studio, then `node tools/farm-look-bake.mjs`, then release the Companion.
  - What goes round the room is cut to your cup and belly size (about 80 characters) instead of the whole look. Only someone who brought their own studio settings sends theirs whole.
  - It's only sent when something actually changes, and a Companion doesn't redraw somebody whose size didn't change.
  - **The bot's own account draws nobody** and sends nothing about bodies, so the bot's PC isn't drawing everyone in the room on top of running the farm.
  - Players can switch drawing back on once they have 0.44.0.
- **Bot 0.50.0 + Companion 0.43.0: Oct 26 batch.** Laynie, Oct 26: ?stuck teleports, kitty outfits/sounds, ratsluts, help leaking the private add-on, ?wash fitted, a seed-milking switch, friend the bot or come off the books, and "sometimes the outfit doesnt apply on contract signing".
  - **?stuck** always moves them to the `rescue` spot straight away; staff are told where they went.
  - **Smart species match** (shared/species.js): kitty, kitten, catgirl, neko → kitty outfit and cat sounds; hucow → cow; ratslut, mouse, rattie → rat. Contracts, outfits and production all read it the same way.
  - **Ratsluts**: new kind (big litters, light milk) with 5 starter outfits (Gutter Sweetheart, Sewer Buck, Midden Queen, Squeak Toy, Hayloft Nibbler), mouse ears, rat tail, livestock tag. Contract speech: Mouse.
  - **?help / ?addons** never show (or guess) an add-on the asker can't use, so the private add-on stays invisible.
  - **?wash** lines are tagged and picked by what the person is: kind, body, on all fours / bound / gagged / caged / belted, latex, pregnant, and which messes are actually on them.
  - **?seedmilk on|off** (also `?milkable seed off`, Companion switch "Milk my seed"): milked up top but nobody, no trap and no ?collect touches their seed.
  - **Friend the bot**: someone on the books, in the room, who hasn't friended the bot gets a private reminder every 6 hours. 48 hours after the first one, still not friends, they're taken off the books (paperwork archived, staff told). Staff and proprietors only get reminders.
  - **Every kind has its own outfits now** (Laynie: "yes, i think i do"): 7 new sets × 5 genders, so 90 starter outfits in all. Bull (black and cognac, dark horns, cowbell, nose ring, cuffed and hobbled), Horse (chestnut and cream show tack, silver bit), Dog (tan petsuit, cream paws, bone tag), Wolf (storm grey and silver petsuit), Cat (black cat in wine and plum), Rabbit (mint see-through bunny suit on pet legs), Sheep (cream and lilac, curled horns, hoof mittens, pet legs). Each has a name and blurb (`?outfit starters`, `?outfit about wolf female`). A proprietor's own saved outfit still always wins.
  - **The outfit wheel** (Laynie: "build them into a new wheel command with a randomized timer for each outfit between 5 minutes and 4 hours"): `?outfitwheel` (or `?wheel outfit`) spins any of the 90 starter outfits for your body, puts it on at once and keeps it on for a random 5 minutes to 4 hours under the farm's padlocks. `?outfitwheel status` shows the time left. Staff can `?outfitwheel <who>` (they get the Yes / Not now card first) and `?outfitwheel stop <who>`. When the time's up the farm opens its own padlocks and puts them back in what they wore before; the safeword ends it at once. Never for anybody on `?ownoutfit` unless they spin it themselves.
  - **Wrong Barn** now dresses them in their new animal's outfit for the hour too, and back when it wears off (with the Companion; not for anybody on `?ownoutfit`).
  - **Companion 0.43.0**: "change back" can open the farm's own padlocks on the pieces the farm put on (only when the farm says so: the wheel or Wrong Barn ending). Nothin' anybody else locked is touched.
  - **Parasite Suckers and Trough Leeches** now put on the **Forbidden Chastity Bra** instead of milking cups (Companion 0.43.0; an older Companion just skips the piece).
  - **Every species checked** (Laynie: "double check all the available species for any missing things like that"). Gaps filled: stall moans for bunny, rabbit, fox, wolf, goat, sheep, rat; stall traits for bunnies, goats/sheep, rats (wolves and foxes now get the canine ones); barn life eat/drink/groom lines for bull, sheep, fox, wolf, deer, goblin, rat, and pups/kitties/ponies now use their cousin's lines instead of the plain ones; rat lines for conditioning, glory stalls, shows, tease, corruption voice, seed taste, Moo Juice; the corruption voice and tease read "catgirl"-style names right; the application understands neko, vixen, kitsune, ponygirl, wolfgirl and the like; "pirate" no longer counts as a rat or "scatterbrain" as a cat. A test now checks every kind × every gender has an outfit, its own contract sound and wash lines.
  - **Contract outfit fix**: the outfit was marked "done" the moment a contract was signed, even if the Companion wasn't on, was offline, or the game refused it, so it never tried again. Now it's only marked done when the Companion says it's on; with no Companion it waits and goes on when their Companion checks in (up to a week, 3 tries); a contract signed outside the bot's books still dresses them; staff hear when there's no outfit for that kind or the game refuses it. **Companion 0.43.0** tells the bot when an outfit failed.
- **Bot 0.49.1: contract rules.** Laynie, Oct 26: "all contracts need the forbid freeing self rules, and locks cannot be picked rule. and any contracts that are permanent need the forbid leaving room rule".
  - Every new contract the farm writes has **Forbid freeing self** (low-difficulty items included) and **Force 'Locks on you can't be picked'** (put back how it was when the contract ends).
  - **Permanent** contracts also have **Forbid leaving the room**, and their terms say so. Roam passes don't apply on one: `?roam start` says why and spends nothing.
  - Only contracts offered from now on get these; ones already signed keep the rules they were signed with.

- **Bot 0.49.0 + Companion 0.42.0: symbiotes sooner, and the latex traps.** Laynie, Oct 25: "can you check the symbiotic cool downs, its a little long - also need to make sure every symbiotic is actually attainable, or atleast have a command way to give them. need more traps for the latex".
  - **Shorter waits.** A symbiote grows a stage every 45 minutes (was 90). After one leaves, the next can find them in 15 minutes if they sent it off or it finished (was 1 hour), 2 hours if it starved (was 12), 4 hours after a safeword (was a day).
